## `AFG-4690-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Fumigation-Residual Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Consolidated Cargo X-Ray Security & Hazmat Incompatibility
- **Intro:** Fumigation-Residual Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Consolidated Cargo X-Ray Security & Hazmat Incompatibility — Pre-Calibration Safety Briefing and PPE Verification: Dosimeter reading must be within ±0.01 mSv of zero.
